# G0 safety build review (read-only)

## 1. Correctness bugs in the new paths

No import cycle, missing export, or bad `getClient` use actually breaks boot. `bootSelfTest.js` correctly imports `getClient, query` from `../../db.js` (`server/db.js:129`); the only hazard is the name collision with `connectionManager.js:268`'s DTC `getClient` — same name, different object, purely a readability trap, not a bug. No cycle: nothing imports `bootSelfTest` except `connectionManager`, `setups`, and the tests, and `bootSelfTest` imports only leaf modules.

`sweepEntries` cannot throw out of its own loop: `entryFeedGate` is fully try/caught (`feedFreshness.js:33-40`) and `sweepEntries` itself is `.catch`-wrapped by `sweepRealOrders` (`orderSweep.js:179`). The gate cannot let a stale MARKET entry through on the primary path: `wantsMarketEntry` is applied consistently at both the batch test (`orderSweep.js:248`) and the per-row skip (`orderSweep.js:260`).

Two real, lower-severity issues:
- **Static 15-minute block vs thin Globex.** `FEED_BLOCK_MINUTES = 15` (`feedFreshness.js:11`) is applied to `AMT_TELL_` MARKET entries. The design doc's own open question (a) — "thin overnight Globex can have legitimately sparse 1-minute bars, so a tight rule risks blocking real entries" — is unresolved in this build. A 16-minute-old overnight bar that is perfectly healthy will block a valid MARKET entry.
- **ET/UTC `CURRENT_DATE`.** `ts >= CURRENT_DATE - 5` (`feedFreshness.js:35`) compares naive-ET `ts` to a DB-timezone-derived date. Harmless for a 5-day lookback window, but it is the same latent ET/UTC smell the codebase has burned on before.

The escalation MARKET path (`orderSweep.js:407-419`) has no freshness re-check between cancel-confirm and market resubmit — it relies on `getCurrentPrice` failing closed at cancel time only (`orderSweep.js:351-361`). Pre-existing, outside this diff, but it is a small stale-feed window adjacent to the new gate.

## 2. The self-test

Mutating `process.env.DTC_DLL_LIMIT_USD` across the await is acceptable in the narrow boot context (switch halted, unset limit fails closed, `finally` restores). But there is a hang risk: if `evaluateDailyLossLimit` never resolves (e.g. a DB pool that never times out), the 15 s `Promise.race` (`bootSelfTest.js:97`) returns while the `finally` never runs, so `DTC_DLL_LIMIT_USD` stays **deleted** while `connectOnce()` proceeds. Later entries stay blocked (fail-closed, safe), but other readers such as `overnightOrderflowEntryDetector.js:82` call `capStopToDailyLimit(..., Number(undefined))` → `NaN` during that window. Also note the "SIDE-EFFECT FREE by construction" claim (`bootSelfTest.js:3`) is imprecise: `persist()` (`bootSelfTest.js:79-85`) writes a real, non-rolled-back `live_attestations` row. It cannot touch the kill switch (only `ks.getState()`), cannot write orders (the `gated_candidates` insert is rolled back, and `finally` runs `ROLLBACK`+`release`), so no transaction leak.

The 15 s fail-closed-on-timeout is correct. **But a late-finishing check does mutate `lastResult` after the timeout.** `body` keeps pushing into `out` after `Promise.race` resolves (`bootSelfTest.js:90-102`); `result.checks` and `lastResult.checks` reference that same live array, so `getBootSelfTestState().checks` can grow post-hoc and diverge from the already-frozen `failures`/`passed`. `passed` cannot flip (it is already false on timeout), so no false re-arm, but the state is not a stable snapshot.

## 3. Auto-rearm interaction

The gate covers the **only** automatic arm caller: `killSwitch.arm('auto-rearm-on-simulation-boot')` at `connectionManager.js:157`, gated at `connectionManager.js:147`. The manual route `POST /sierra-chart/start` (`server/routes/sierraChart.js:101`) is **not** gated — by design (human override), but it means a hasty operator can re-arm a system whose self-test just failed, protected only by the banner.

On a clean failure, visibility is good: log (`bootSelfTest.js:104`, `connectionManager.js:148`), `live_attestations` DIVERGENCE row (`bootSelfTest.js:82-84`), and the banner (`quick-check.html:4260`).

**The gap:** if `runBootSelfTest` throws before setting `lastResult` (caught at `connectionManager.js:115`), `lastResult` stays `null`, `bootSelfTestAllowsRearm()` returns false (correct fail-closed), but the banner shows **nothing** — `quick-check.html:4260` only fires on `passed === false`, and `bootSelfTest` is `null` (silent per the comment at 4259). `live_attestations` also has no row, and invariant [27] merely `warn`s on a missing row (`test_invariants.mjs:2074`). So a crashed (not failed) self-test strands the system halted all day with only the generic "DISARMED (process restart)" banner and one console line — exactly the "no visible reason" scenario.

## 4. Test quality

`test_g0_safety.mjs` proves the gate *functions* behave and that the strings are *wired*, not that the integration works: the sweep wiring is regex-based (`test_g0_safety.mjs:51-54`, invariant `test_invariants.mjs:2064-2070`), so a refactor that keeps the substrings but breaks the skip would pass. No test actually runs `sweepEntries` against a stale feed and asserts a `recordEntrySkip` row.

Missing edge cases: thin overnight Globex bars near 15 min; the 17:00–18:00 maintenance gap and the ~18:00 reopen where the last bar (16:59) is 60+ min old; a bar timestamp across the DST repeated/ambiguous hour (`etNaiveTimestampToMs` → `etNaiveStringToUtcIso`, `sierraParser.js:274-289`, is DST-correct but untested); and the future-tolerance boundary (−1 vs −2.1 min). `dllUnsetLimitBlocks` and `rolledBackInsertWorks` are **not** mutation-tested — only `ks`/`eligible` are injectable (`bootSelfTest.js:50`) — so the tests run those two checks but never prove they would fail the self-test if broken. Finally, the header "touches no live state" (`test_g0_safety.mjs:1-2`) is inaccurate: `dllUnsetLimitBlocks` mutates `process.env` and `rolledBackInsertWorks` does a real DB round trip (rolled back).

## The 3 most important changes
1. Make a crashed/unfinished self-test **visible**: surface `bootSelfTest: {state:'NOT_RUN'/'CRASHED'}` distinctly instead of silent `null` in the banner/state, so a failed boot cannot strand the system halted with no on-screen reason.
2. Fix the `runBootSelfTest` timeout race so a late-finishing check cannot mutate `out`/`lastResult` after the result is set (snapshot the array, or have `body` stop pushing once timed out).
3. Reconcile the static 15-minute MARKET block with thin-Globex reality (data-derived threshold or an explicit test/justification), and add tests for the 17:00–18:00 reopen and DST-boundary bar parsing.
